        Engineering brief produced from the codebase and task details:

        ### Task Summary
        Refactor admin_chat to route messages by author identity (admin→agent, pending-recipient→state machine, else→passthrough), add a new `awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation` intent state with keyword-based confirm/negative/ambiguous parsing, add query_*/initiate_batch_payment/upsert_wallet_for_user agent tools, introduce an admin-DM-only AdminApprovalView approval gate for real admin_chat payments (replacing PaymentConfirmView for that producer only), auto-skip the test phase for verified wallets, and add a 24h timeout escalation. Grants flow and all hardening/cleanup/polish invariants stay intact.

        ### Key Evidence
        - Today admin_chat routing is content-based: on_message first calls _check_pending_payment_reply which intercepts any recipient reply in any channel for any active intent, then falls through to mention/DM detection and agent invocation. Identity routing replaces both branches with a single author-id switch. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:790-835 (on_message), :563-640 (_check_pending_payment_reply); relevance: high)
- Current intent status CHECK constraint is ('awaiting_wallet','awaiting_test','awaiting_confirmation','confirmed','completed','failed','cancelled'). No 'awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation' exists; a SQL migration must add it to the CHECK constraint. (source: sql/admin_payment_intents.sql:28-37; relevance: high)
- Today's flow creates a real test payment and AUTO-confirms it via PaymentActorKind.AUTO in _start_admin_payment_flow, then on terminal test success creates the real payment and calls PaymentUICog.send_confirmation_request for recipient confirmation. The new flow must instead park the intent in awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation after the test confirms on-chain, then wait for the recipient's text ack before creating the real payment. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:234-309 (_start_admin_payment_flow, _handle_confirmation_received) and :311-414 (handle_payment_result test branch); relevance: high)
- PaymentConfirmView is used for BOTH grants and admin_chat real payments today — both grants_cog.py:719 and admin_chat_cog.py:393 call payment_ui_cog.send_confirmation_request. The refactor must NOT drop the grants call site, only the admin_chat call site. (source: src/features/grants/grants_cog.py:719, src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:393; relevance: high)
- Reply classification today uses a Claude-based _classify_payment_reply call per message; the new pending-recipient state machine must be deterministic with zero LLM calls. The task mandates hardcoded regex/keyword matching for both wallet detection and confirmation keywords. Supabase .insert([list]) is atomic per-request on PostgREST but only within one table — sufficient here since all rows are admin_payment_intents.         ### Constraints
        - MUST NOT touch solana_client.py / solana_provider.py — the rebroadcast loop, priority fees, status.err handling all stay.
- MUST NOT touch grants_cog. Grants keeps its own flow (test auto-confirmed by bot, recipient clicks PaymentConfirmView button). grants_cog.py:719 call to send_confirmation_request MUST remain functional.
- MUST preserve hardening invariants: per-user-per-day caps, canonical wallet swap defense, idempotency collision detection, WalletUpdateBlockedError, RLS comments, prompt-injection hardening (wallet lookups only from wallet_registry), null-recipient fail-closed in PaymentService.confirm_payment.
- MUST preserve cleanup invariants: PaymentActor/PRODUCER_FLOWS registry, tx_signature_history column, PaymentWorkerCog/PaymentUICog split, safe_delete_messages primitive.
- MUST preserve polish invariants: reconcile_with_chain, dynamic priority fees, /payment-resolve slash command, provider name migration, docs/payments.md, docs/runbook-payments.md, scripts/check_payment_invariants.py, structured tx_confirm_decision logging.
- Agent MUST NEVER accept LLM-sourced wallet addresses. initiate_batch_payment and upsert_wallet_for_user must reuse the existing 'pop and warn' guard pattern from execute_initiate_payment at tools.py:2501-2508 (for the batch), and upsert_wallet_for_user must take the wallet_address explicitly from a validated admin parameter (not from unrelated LLM context).
- verified_at MUST only be set after a real round-trip: on-chain test confirmation + recipient text acknowledgment. Neither alone is sufficient. upsert_wallet_for_user creates the row with verified_at=NULL.
- Admin DM approval gate fires ONLY for real (is_test=False) payments under producer='admin_chat'. NEVER for is_test=True. NEVER for producer='grants'.
- One admin DM per payment (not per batch). A batch of 16 → 16 DMs. Each DM carries its own AdminApprovalView with a unique custom_id.
- AdminApprovalView must be persistent (timeout=None) and re-registered on bot startup via cog_load for restart recovery. Pattern: match PaymentConfirmView registration in payment_ui_cog.py.
- PaymentConfirmView must NO LONGER fire for producer='admin_chat' real payments. Grants keeps using it. The gating should be at the call site in admin_chat_cog.py (remove/replace the send_confirmation_request call) — do NOT add a producer check inside send_confirmation_request because that would couple PaymentUICog to producer semantics.
- Existing admin_payment_intents schema: a SQL migration must add 'awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation' and 'awaiting_admin_approval' to the status CHECK constraint; migration must be idempotent (DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS / ADD CONSTRAINT) and ship with the code change.
- Identity router for approved-member messages: under the new routing, approved members' messages are no longer forwarded to the agent unless they are the admin. Document explicitly — this is an intentional scoping reduction per the task spec. MEMBER_SYSTEM_PROMPT is NOT deleted (dead code left in place).
- Keyword lists for confirmation/negative/ambiguous MUST be hardcoded in code (no LLM call). Case-insensitive match, whole-word boundary preferred, include emoji variants ('👍'). Ambiguous = neither positive nor negative.
- 24h timeout: new background task (add to AdminChatCog or PaymentWorkerCog) that scans admin_payment_intents with status=awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation AND updated_at < now-24h, transitions to manual_review, and DMs admin. Interval ≥ 5 minutes to avoid DB hammering.
- Batch tool atomicity: prefer Supabase .insert([list]) (PostgREST atomic per-request) for create_admin_payment_intents_batch. If any row fails validation BEFORE insert, reject the whole batch with zero DB writes. If the DB rejects the multi-row insert, the whole request fails atomically.
- Test churn expected: test_admin_payments.py (1350 lines) will see broad rewrites. New tests MUST NOT mock _classify_payment_reply (it's no longer called from the state machine path). They SHOULD continue to assert WalletUpdateBlockedError paths.
- DO NOT add a cog-layer rejection of agent replies that make state claims without query-tool calls. Per the task: system-prompt guidance only, no enforcement.
- DO NOT start nested megaplan plans or invoke the megaplan CLI from within implementation — treat megaplan mentions as context only.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Land this in phased, reviewable chunks that keep tests green at every step.

PHASE 1 — Schema + state enum (smallest diff, lowest risk):
(a) SQL migration: extend admin_payment_intents status CHECK to add 'awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation' and 'awaiting_admin_approval'. Idempotent DROP/ADD CONSTRAINT pattern. Update sql/admin_payment_intents.sql + any migration runner.
(b) db_handler helpers: add create_admin_payment_intents_batch (Supabase .insert([list])), a list_intents_by_status helper, a list_stale_test_receipt_intents(cutoff) for the 24h sweep, and an ambiguous-reply counter update path.

PHASE 2 — Identity router (on_message rewrite):
Replace the current on_message + _check_pending_payment_reply branching with a single switch:
 1. If author.id == ADMIN_USER_ID → existing agent path (unchanged except new tools added in phase 4).
 2. Else if db_handler.get_active_intent_for_recipient returns a row → dispatch to a new _handle_pending_recipient_message(intent, message) that routes by intent.status (awaiting_wallet, awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation). No LLM calls.
 3. Else → return without handling (fallthrough).
Delete the Claude-based _classify_payment_reply code path from the message-handler flow (keep the function around for the phase-2 diff small; delete in a later cleanup commit if truly unused).
Add deterministic helpers:
 - `_parse_wallet_from_text(content) -> str | None` reusing is_valid_solana_address.
 - `_classify_confirmation(content) -> Literal['positive','negative','ambiguous']` with hardcoded keyword lists from the task spec (whole-word, case-insensitive, emoji-aware).

PHASE 3 — Flow rewiring (test-receipt + admin-DM gate):
(a) Modify handle_payment_result's test-success branch: instead of creating the final payment inline, transition intent to awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation, post the 'please check your wallet and reply confirmed' prompt in the thread, store the prompt_message_id.
(b) When _handle_pending_recipient_message gets a positive confirmation in awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation: call db_handler.mark_wallet_verified, create the real payment via payment_service.request_payment (is_test=False), transition intent to awaiting_admin_approval, and fire the admin DM.
(c) Auto-skip path: in execute_initiate_payment (and execute_initiate_batch_payment), when a verified wallet exists, create the real payment directly and go straight to awaiting_admin_approval — skip _start_admin_payment_flow (which is test-only). This is a branch on wallet_record.verified_at at intent-creation time, not inside _start_admin_payment_flow.
(d) Admin DM: new helper `_send_admin_approval_dm(payment)` in PaymentUICog that DMs ADMIN_USER_ID with amount/recipient/redacted-wallet/intent_id/jump-link and attaches a new AdminApprovalView keyed by payment_id. On discord.Forbidden → log ERROR, leave intent in awaiting_admin_approval (fail-closed, no public fallback).
(e) New AdminApprovalView class in payment_ui_cog.py, pattern-copied from PaymentConfirmView. Button callback verifies interaction.user.id == ADMIN_USER_ID, calls payment_service.confirm_payment, edits the DM to ✅, follows up with 'payment confirmed, sending', posts 'Payment sent to @user' in the intent's thread, and deletes stale flow messages via safe_delete_messages.
(f) Persistence: PaymentUICog.cog_load gains a _register_pending_admin_approval_views pass that queries admin_payment_intents WHERE status='awaiting_admin_approval' and bot.add_view(AdminApprovalView(...)) for each.
(g) Remove the admin_chat_cog.py:393 send_confirmation_request call. Grants' call at grants_cog.py:719 stays untouched.

PHASE 4 — New agent tools:
(a) Three read-only query tools (query_payment_state, query_wallet_state, list_recent_payments): thin wrappers over existing db_handler helpers with wallet redaction applied via the existing redact_wallet import. Add to TOOLS list in tools.py, dispatch in execute_tool, and list in SYSTEM_PROMPT.
(b) initiate_batch_payment: takes payments=[{recipient_user_id, amount_sol, reason?}, ...]. Per-payment LLM-wallet-injection guard (pop wallet_address/recipient_wallet and warn, same as current execute_initiate_payment). Validate every element first (guild_id, channel_id, amount > 0, no active duplicate for recipient), build the payload list, call db_handler.create_admin_payment_intents_batch. On success, iterate the returned rows and fan out through the same per-payment mechanics as initiate_payment (auto-skip branch for verified wallets OR _start_admin_payment_flow for unverified). Each verified path fires its own admin DM — so a 16-payment batch produces 16 DMs.
(c) upsert_wallet_for_user: takes (user_id, wallet_address, chain='solana', reason?). Admin-only check via a requester context flag. Validate wallet_address via is_valid_solana_address. Call db_handler.upsert_wallet (propagating WalletUpdateBlockedError to the admin reply). Never sets verified_at. On success, DM the admin: 'wallet for @user set to {redacted}, will be verified on next payment'.
(d) SYSTEM_PROMPT addition: a new 'State questions → query first' section instructing the agent to call query_payment_state / query_wallet_state / list_recent_payments before claiming state. Soft guidance, not enforcement. Add the five new tool lines to the 'Doing things' section of the prompt (3 query + 1 batch + 1 upsert).

PHASE 5 — 24h timeout sweep:
New background task (recommend AdminChatCog, since it owns intent lifecycle). @tasks.loop(minutes=15) scans admin_payment_intents WHERE status='awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation' AND updated_at < now()-interval '24 hours', transitions each to manual_review, and DMs admin with full context (intent_id, recipient, tx_signature, wallet).

PHASE 6 — Tests:
Rewrite test_admin_payments.py broadly: new router tests, state-machine tests, keyword parser tests (parametrized), batch atomicity test, upsert admin-check test, auto-skip test, AdminApprovalView persistence + click test, admin DM fail-closed test, gated PaymentConfirmView test, 24h timeout test. Keep pass_to_pass tests (payment state machine, race, reconcile, authorization, solana client, safe delete, invariants, tx signature history) untouched; verify they stay green.

PHASE 7 — Docs:
Append to docs/payments.md and docs/runbook-payments.md: identity routing overview, new states, admin DM approval gate, auto-skip rule, batch tool semantics, upsert tool semantics, and the scoping-reduction note for approved members.

Flagged risks / open questions the plan step should resolve:
 - Batch atomicity when Supabase multi-insert partially fails: confirm PostgREST behavior (should be atomic per-request). If not, fall back to per-row inserts inside a new SQL RPC function wrapped in a txn.
 - The prompt_message_id column already exists on admin_payment_intents but currently tracks only one prompt; the new flow posts TWO prompts (wallet request + 'please check and reply confirmed'). Consider adding a second column (e.g., receipt_prompt_message_id) or reusing prompt_message_id by overwriting — prefer reuse with a comment, since only the latest is needed for safe-delete.
 - The ambiguous-reply counter needs a new integer column (e.g., ambiguous_reply_count default 0) on admin_payment_intents, or store it in a JSONB metadata column if one exists. Verify whether metadata exists; if not, add the integer column in the same migration.
 - AdminApprovalView custom_id collision: use f'payment_admin_approve:{payment_id}' — must not collide with the existing f'payment_confirm:{payment_id}' (they don't, but grep to confirm).

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Identity-based routing for admin_chat with pending-recipient state machine, batch payment creation, admin DM approval gate, auto-skip test for verified wallets, and wallet upsert tooling.

ARCHITECTURAL CHANGE — identity-based router replaces content-based message classification. After this megaplan lands, admin_chat message handling routes entirely by WHO the author is, not by WHAT they said:

1. If author is ADMIN_USER_ID → full agent path (Claude with all tools including new ones). The agent has access to query tools (new, read-only) and action tools (new initiate_payment batch variant, new upsert_wallet_for_user). System prompt softly guides the agent to use query tools for state questions (not strict enforcement — the admin can correct hallucinations; money cannot move without an explicit tool call so the safety is structural).

2. If author has an active non-terminal row in admin_payment_intents (a pending payment recipient) → pure state machine. No LLM in the loop. The state machine reads the message text and routes based on the intent's current state. Two states matter:
   - awaiting_wallet: parse message for a Solana-address-shaped string (base58, 32-44 chars, proper byte length). If found, register the wallet (verified_at=NULL), trigger test payment. If not found, ignore silently (no spamming).
   - awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation (NEW STATE): parse message for confirmation keywords ('confirmed', 'received', 'got it', 'yes', 'yep', 'confirm', '👍'), negative keywords ('no', 'didnt', 'not received', 'missing', 'nothing'), or ambiguous. On confirmation → mark wallet_registry.verified_at=now, transition intent to queue the real payment. On negative → tag admin in thread with full context (tx signature, recipient wallet, intent id). On ambiguous → ignore the first ambiguous message; tag admin on the second.

3. Otherwise → fall through (the admin_chat cog does nothing; other cogs like grants/gating/logging handle what they handle).

RECIPIENT-FACING FLOW CHANGES:

- First-ever payment to a new wallet: recipient posts wallet (1 text interaction) → bot sends 0.002 SOL test → bot posts 'Please check your wallet and reply confirmed when you see it' → recipient replies with confirmation text (1 text interaction) → state machine marks wallet verified → real payment auto-queues → admin DM approval gate fires → admin clicks → real payment sends. RECIPIENT TOTAL: 2 text interactions.
- Subsequent payments to a now-verified wallet: skip test entirely. Bot creates real payment → admin DM approval → admin clicks → send. RECIPIENT TOTAL: 0 interactions.
- Drop the existing PaymentConfirmView button for admin_chat real payments. The admin DM is now the sole real-payment gate. PaymentUICog.send_confirmation_request should no longer fire for producer='admin_chat' real payments. It still fires for grants (grants is unaffected by this megaplan; keeps its own flow).

NEW TOOLS FOR THE ADMIN AGENT:

A) query_payment_state(payment_id=None, user_id=None) → returns canonical DB state for a payment or all recent payments for a user. Read-only.
B) query_wallet_state(user_id) → returns wallet_registry rows for a user (address, verified_at, created_at). Read-only.
C) list_recent_payments(producer=None, user_id=None, limit=20) → returns recent payment_requests rows with redacted wallets. Read-only.
D) initiate_batch_payment(payments=[{recipient_user_id, amount_sol, reason?}, ...]) → creates N admin_payment_intents atomically in a single DB transaction. Each flows through the normal per-payment mechanical pipeline: if wallet unverified, runs the test → receipt confirmation cycle; once verified, real payment auto-queues; admin DM approval gate fires individually PER payment (one DM per payment, not per batch). If any intent creation fails, roll back all.
E) upsert_wallet_for_user(user_id, wallet_address, chain='solana', reason?) → admin-initiated wallet registration. Creates or updates wallet_registry with verified_at=NULL (verification still requires a test payment round-trip). On success, DMs the admin with a confirmation receipt ('wallet for @user set to 8ThP...xyz1, will be verified on next payment'). Does NOT mark verified_at.

ADMIN DM APPROVAL GATE:

- Fires ONLY for real (is_test=False) payments under producer='admin_chat'. Grants is unaffected.
- One DM per payment (even for batches — 16 payments = 16 DMs). The admin clicks Confirm on each.
- DM message format: clear amount, recipient, wallet (redacted), intent_id, jump link to original thread.
- On admin click: edit original DM to '✅ approved', send follow-up DM 'payment confirmed, sending', post reply in the intent's thread 'Payment sent to @user', delete stale flow messages in the thread via the existing safe_delete_messages primitive.
- New AdminApprovalView persistent view class, registered on bot startup for recovery across restarts.
- If admin DM delivery fails (discord.Forbidden, rate limit) → fail-closed, payment stays in awaiting_admin_approval, log at ERROR level. Do not auto-fall-back to public channel (assumption: DM delivery is reliable in practice; failure is rare enough to be a manual-ops event).

AUTO-SKIP TEST FOR VERIFIED WALLETS:

- When the admin initiates a payment via initiate_payment or initiate_batch_payment, and the recipient has a wallet_registry row with verified_at IS NOT NULL, skip the test payment creation entirely.
- Go straight to creating the real payment row, auto-queue, trigger admin DM approval.
- First-time and verified paths share the admin DM approval gate.

HALLUCINATION FIX (softer than originally proposed):

- Add the three query tools (query_payment_state, query_wallet_state, list_recent_payments) to the agent's tool palette.
- Update the admin agent's system prompt with a new section: 'For questions about the state of payments or wallets, call query_payment_state / query_wallet_state / list_recent_payments FIRST. Do not claim state from memory or channel context.'
- This is guidance, not enforcement. The agent CAN still hallucinate, but the operator (the admin) is the only audience, can correct, and money cannot move without an explicit tool call (which is visible and auditable).
- Do NOT add a cog-layer rejection of replies that make state claims without query tool calls — the user specifically said not to require tool calls.

WHAT THIS MEGAPLAN IS NOT TOUCHING (preserve existing invariants):

- solana_client.py / solana_provider.py — the rebroadcast loop, priority fees, status.err check, RPC unreachable handling all stay.
- Hardening pass inventory: per-user-per-day caps, canonical wallet swap defense, idempotency collision detection, WalletUpdateBlockedError, RLS comment, prompt-injection hardening in admin_chat/tools.py (wallet lookups only from wallet_registry), null-recipient fail-closed in PaymentService.confirm_payment.
- Cleanup inventory: PaymentActor/PRODUCER_FLOWS registry, tx_signature_history column, PaymentWorkerCog + PaymentUICog split, safe_delete_messages primitive.
- Polish inventory: reconcile_with_chain, dynamic priority fees, /payment-resolve slash command, provider name migration, docs/payments.md, docs/runbook-payments.md, scripts/check_payment_invariants.py, structured tx_confirm_decision logging.
- Grants cog — completely unaffected. Grants uses its own thread-scoped listener, has its own flow (test auto-confirmed by bot, recipient clicks button for real payment), and this megaplan does not touch it.
- The 'approved members can chat with bot' feature — the _can_user_message_bot path in admin_chat_cog. Under identity-based routing, approved-member messages are no longer forwarded to the agent unless the member is ALSO the ADMIN_USER_ID. This is an intentional scoping decision — the admin is the sole human who can conversationally interact with the agent. Document this in the plan explicitly so no one is surprised.

SUCCESS CRITERIA:

- Identity router correctly classifies admin, pending recipient, and fallthrough based solely on author identity.
- Pending recipient state machine handles awaiting_wallet and awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation deterministically with no LLM calls.
- Test-receipt confirmation flow: recipient's text acknowledgment is the sole gate for marking wallet_registry.verified_at (not on-chain confirmation alone).
- Negative / ambiguous recipient responses escalate to the admin via thread tag, not silent failure.
- 24-hour timeout on awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation → escalate to manual_review + admin DM.
- New tools (query_*, initiate_batch_payment, upsert_wallet_for_user) are admin-only and correctly scoped.
- initiate_batch_payment creates all intents atomically or none (DB transaction).
- Auto-skip test path correctly bypasses the test phase for already-verified wallets.
- Admin DM approval gate fires exactly once per real admin_chat payment, never for tests, never for grants.
- AdminApprovalView is persistent-safe across bot restarts.
- PaymentConfirmView button no longer fires for admin_chat real payments (grants-only now).
- No regression in existing hardening/cleanup/polish invariants — all existing tests pass.
- New tests cover: identity router branches, state machine state transitions, wallet regex parsing, confirmation keyword matching, batch tool atomicity, upsert_wallet_for_user admin check, auto-skip verified path, admin DM approval view persistence and click handling.

  "meta_commentary": "Execute phase-by-phase per the plan's execution order: T1\u2192T2 (schema+helpers), T3+T4 (payment policy+restart filter), T5\u2192T7 (parsers+router+dispatch), T8\u2192T11 (flow rewiring + gate helpers + auto-skip), T12\u2192T15+T18 (tools), T16+T17 (sweeps), T19 (tests), T20 (validation). Tasks T3, T5, T12, T14, T18 have no dependencies and can be done in any order or in parallel within a phase.\n\nThe single highest-leverage execution-time addition is the @tasks.loop(minutes=5) periodic orphan sweep in T16 \u2014 the gate marked this as a required directive in its warnings (closes FLAG-017 and the iteration-5 callers/correctness/all_locations/scope flags). Without it, stale awaiting_admin_init intents only clear at restart, which is unacceptable because they block fresh initiations for the affected recipient via has_active_payment_or_intent / get_active_intent_for_recipient.\n\nThe Rev-5 design is built around ONE critical insight: the orphan sweep MUST live in PaymentUICog itself, not AdminChatCog. Do not be tempted to move it back \u2014 main.py loads PaymentUICog before AdminChatCog and tests instantiate PaymentUICog with no admin_chat_cog at all. The dedicated regression test test_payment_ui_cog_loads_without_admin_chat_cog (T19) catches accidental regression on this.\n\nTwo helpers, one flow each: _gate_existing_intent (T9) is shared by test-receipt-positive AND batch verified fan-out \u2014 the intent already exists in a non-terminal state. _gate_fresh_intent_atomic (T9) is ONLY for the single execute_initiate_payment verified path \u2014 no intent yet, atomic create with status+final_payment_id in one insert. Do not cross these wires.\n\nThe atomicity contract for initiate_batch_payment (T13) is the single DB call. After that call, fan-out is per-entry and non-atomic by necessity (each entry needs its own payment_requests row); the orphan sweep is the recovery mechanism for mid-fan-out crashes. The brief's fail_to_pass test test_initiate_batch_payment_atomic_all_or_none asserts this exact contract.\n\nWhen implementing T19, expect to delete a substantial portion of test_admin_payments.py \u2014 every test that mocks _classify_payment_reply or asserts the awaiting_confirmation\u2192final-payment transition is now obsolete. Don't try to preserve them. Replace with the listed new tests.\n\nmanual_review stays BLOCKING. Several earlier iterations tried to make it non-blocking and the gate reverted that change. The admin clears it via resolve_admin_intent which cascade-cancels. Do not touch get_active_intent_for_recipient / list_active_intents / has_active_payment_or_intent.\n\nThe dead-code preservation matters: MEMBER_SYSTEM_PROMPT, _can_user_message_bot, _is_directed_at_bot stay in place but become unreachable. Document with a brief comment, do not delete.\n\nFinal test phase (T20): if anything fails, read the error and fix the root cause. Do NOT skip, xfail, or weaken assertions to make tests pass. The throwaway repro script should exercise both the first-payment round-trip (unverified\u2192test\u2192ack\u2192verified\u2192real\u2192admin DM) and the verified-wallet single-initiate atomic path \u2014 these are the two most-critical flows.",

        {
  "version": 5,

  "hash": "sha256:b88c620f5548ed9e09d3180f0769f5af96879d79d9ea347ad02dad8a86dd14cc",
  "changes_summary": "Revision 5 applies two surgical fixes to resolve the iteration-4 concerns: (1) **Inverted the orphan-sweep dependency** \u2014 moved `_reconcile_admin_chat_orphans` OUT of `AdminChatCog` and INTO `PaymentUICog` itself. The sweep only needs `self.db_handler`, `self.payment_service`, `self.bot`, and `self._send_admin_approval_dm`, all already accessible from the UI cog. This eliminates the cross-cog ordering problem entirely: no `bot.admin_chat_cog.reconcile_orphan_payments_for_ui_cog()` accessor, no `main.py` bootstrap reorder, no test harness changes, no `FakeBot` additions. Added a critical regression test `test_payment_ui_cog_loads_without_admin_chat_cog` in `test_payment_cog_split.py` that validates `PaymentUICog.cog_load()` works with a `FakeBot` that has no `admin_chat_cog` attribute. Closes FLAG-015, correctness (v4), all_locations (v4), callers (v4). (2) **Restored unified atomic batch semantics via a new transient `awaiting_admin_init` state.** All `initiate_batch_payment` entries (verified + unverified) now go through ONE `create_admin_payment_intents_batch` call \u2014 true all-or-none single-DB-call semantics matching the brief's fail_to_pass test. Verified entries are inserted with `status='awaiting_admin_init'`; unverified with `status='awaiting_wallet'`. `awaiting_admin_init` is added to the CHECK constraint (Step 1), silently ignored by the state-machine dispatch (Step 7 \u2014 new unit test `test_state_machine_silently_ignores_awaiting_admin_init` asserts posting a Solana address never reopens the test path), and handled by the orphan sweep as a new case (e): stale `awaiting_admin_init` intents older than 5 minutes with no `final_payment_id` are cancelled (fail-closed, no payment_requests cleanup needed because no payment row was ever created). After the atomic batch insert succeeds, verified entries iterate through the existing `_gate_existing_intent` helper (unified with the test-receipt-positive path) to create their payment and transition to `awaiting_admin_approval`. Added `test_initiate_batch_payment_verified_fan_out_transitions_to_awaiting_admin_approval` and `test_initiate_batch_payment_fan_out_crash_leaves_awaiting_admin_init_for_sweep`. Closes FLAG-016, issue_hints (v4), scope (v4). `_gate_fresh_intent_atomic` stays in place for the SINGLE `initiate_payment` verified path (no batch wrapper); batch exclusively uses `_gate_existing_intent` for fan-out. No changes to Phases 1-3 structure, no changes to `main.py`, no changes to `test_payment_cog_split.py` or `test_scheduler.py` harnesses.",

  "questions": [
    "For the `awaiting_admin_init` staleness cutoff (5 minutes): is this a reasonable trade-off between fast crash recovery and avoiding false positives on a transient startup delay? I'm assuming 5 minutes is the right middle ground. Confirm.",
    "The `test_initiate_batch_payment_fan_out_crash_leaves_awaiting_admin_init_for_sweep` test simulates a mid-fan-out crash by mocking `request_payment` to raise.     "Orphan-payment reconciliation sweep lives in `PaymentUICog._reconcile_admin_chat_orphans` \u2014 not in `AdminChatCog`.     "`initiate_batch_payment` uses a unified single atomic `create_admin_payment_intents_batch` call for all entries.     "`awaiting_admin_init` is added to the CHECK constraint as a new non-routable state. The state-machine dispatch silently ignores it (same as `awaiting_test`, `awaiting_admin_approval`).     "Stale `awaiting_admin_init` intents are detected by a new `list_stale_awaiting_admin_init_intents(cutoff_iso)` DB helper with a 5-minute staleness cutoff \u2014 short enough to resolve batch fan-out crashes quickly, long enough to survive transient startup delays.",
    "Stale `awaiting_admin_init` intents are cancelled fail-closed. No `payment_requests` cleanup is needed because the payment row is only created by `_gate_existing_intent` AFTER the intent is already in place; if the intent is stuck in `awaiting_admin_init`, no payment row exists yet.",
    "`_gate_existing_intent` is the shared helper used by BOTH the test-receipt-positive handler AND the batch verified-fan-out.     "`_gate_fresh_intent_atomic` stays in place for the SINGLE `execute_initiate_payment` verified path only. It uses the Rev-4 payment-first ordering (atomic insert of intent with both status and final_payment_id in one call) because there's no N>1 atomic-batch requirement for single initiate.",
    "The brief's `test_initiate_batch_payment_atomic_all_or_none` fail_to_pass test is satisfied by Rev-5's unified atomic insert: one failed element \u2192 zero DB writes via validation-phase rejection; valid batch \u2192 all N intent rows persisted in ONE `create_admin_payment_intents_batch` call.",
    "`bot.payment_ui_cog` attribute is set in `PaymentUICog.__init__` (carried over from the existing file). `_gate_existing_intent` in `admin_chat_cog.py` references `bot.payment_ui_cog._send_admin_approval_dm(...)` at runtime, after the UI cog has loaded.",
    "`manual_review` remains BLOCKING; admin clears via `resolve_admin_intent`.",
    "`resolve_admin_intent` cascades cancellation to linked `test_payment_id` and `final_payment_id` via existing `cancel_payment`; refuses non-cancellable linked payments.",
    "`PaymentActorKind.ADMIN_DM` is reused; no new enum value.",

Does the fix handle all of them?: Checked the active-intent callers in `src/common/db_handler.py:1842-1889`. `get_active_intent_for_recipient(...)` and `list_active_intents(...)` treat anything except `completed`, `failed`, and `cancelled` as active, so a new `manual_review` state will continue to block fresh initiations and keep routing recipients into the admin-chat state-machine branch unless a caller-visible resolution path is added.",
      "category": "correctness",
      "severity_hint": "likely-significant",
      "evidence": "Addressed all 19 gate flags in a focused revision (no re-scope). Key fixes: (1) New Step 3 updates `producer_flows.py` to add `ADMIN_DM` to `admin_chat.real_confirmed_by` \u2014 reuses the **existing** `PaymentActorKind.ADMIN_DM` (not a new `ADMIN_CLICK`). (2) New Step 4 filters the `PaymentConfirmView` restart-recovery pass to skip admin_chat real payments via a new db_handler helper. (3) Step 6 drops `_is_directed_at_bot` gating for ADMIN_USER_ID \u2014 admin messages reach the agent unconditionally (plain channel messages included). (4) Step 5 replaces the whitespace-only wallet parser with a tolerant regex that strips markdown/punctuation delimiters before validating candidates. (5) Step 10 click handler posts \"queued for sending\" (not \"Payment sent\") and defers the sent-confirmation to the existing `handle_payment_result` worker-completion path. (6) Step 1.3 adds a second `receipt_prompt_message_id` column so safe-delete can clear BOTH the wallet prompt AND the receipt prompt. (7) Step 13 verified-wallet batch entries now skip `awaiting_test` entirely \u2014 they land directly in `awaiting_admin_approval` at intent-creation time. (8) Step 15 adds the five new tool names to `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS` so the module-level exhaustiveness assert doesn't fire; Step 16 extends `agent.py`'s `admin_user_id` injection to cover `initiate_batch_payment` and `upsert_wallet_for_user`. (9) Step 2.5 excludes `manual_review` from active-intent lookups AND new Step 20 adds a `resolve_admin_intent` tool as the operator resolution surface \u2014 `manual_review` intents no longer block future initiations and admin can cancel/complete them explicitly. (10) Step 1.4 updates the unique partial index to exclude `manual_review`. Rewrote Execution Order note to call out the Phase 2 triple as a single interlocked change. Added new tests for every flag: producer_flows authorization, restart-recovery filter, tolerant wallet parser, \"queued not sent\" click handler, both-prompt safe-delete, batch verified-skip, ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS exhaustiveness, admin_user_id injection for new tools, manual_review non-blocking, resolve_admin_intent.",

  "recommendation": "PROCEED",
  "rationale": "Iteration 5 with score stable at 25.5 (basically unchanged from 26.0 in iter 4), 16 flags resolved cumulatively, plan delta narrowing (90%\u219267%\u219274%\u219263%), zero recurring critiques, and a \"high iteration count\" warning from the gate itself. Every single v1-v4 flag entry is marked `addressed`; the remaining open flags are all aliases of **one genuinely distinct concern**: the periodic scheduling of `_reconcile_admin_chat_orphans` during normal uptime. Seven flag entries (correctness, all_locations, scope, callers, issue_hints, FLAG-017, plus minor alias) all trace back to this single gap: the sweep only runs at `cog_load`, so a mid-fan-out batch crash leaves `awaiting_admin_init` entries stranded until restart. **The fix is trivial** (~10 lines): add `@tasks.loop(minutes=5) _orphan_sweep_loop` to `PaymentUICog` that calls the existing `_reconcile_admin_chat_orphans()`, start in `cog_load` after the initial invocation, stop in `cog_unload`. This is clearly an execution-time detail, not a design-level question \u2014 all the design decisions (dependency inversion, atomic batch insert, transient state, orphan sweep logic) are settled and correct. The issue_hints (v5) \"divergence\" flag is also disputable: the brief explicitly says `initiate_batch_payment` \"creates N admin_payment_intents atomically... each flows through the normal per-payment mechanical pipeline\", which matches the Rev-5 design (atomic insert, then per-entry pipeline). `awaiting_admin_init` is a transient internal state that exists only during the pipeline phase; from the outside observer the intent lands in `awaiting_admin_approval` as required. Further iteration at this point risks introducing new concerns (as iter 2 and iter 4 both demonstrated) without meaningfully improving the outcome. Ship with an explicit execution-time directive to add the periodic loop.",
  "signals_assessment": "Iteration 5, weighted score trajectory 30.0\u219232.0\u219220.5\u219226.0\u219225.5 (stable). Plan delta 62.77% (down from 74%, 90%), continuing to narrow. 16 flags resolved cumulatively across 5 rounds, zero recurring critiques \u2014 the loop is converging. Preflight clean. Gate's own \"high iteration count\" warning indicates the harness is signaling that further rounds may be counterproductive. Remaining 7 open flags all alias a single concrete gap (periodic scheduling of the orphan sweep) with a trivial ~10-line execution-time fix. No debt subsystem overlaps. The plan is ready to execute with explicit directives carried in warnings and settled decisions.",
  "warnings": [
    "Iteration 5: high iteration count.",
    "**EXECUTION-TIME REQUIRED ADDITION (addresses FLAG-017 / correctness / all_locations / callers / scope):** Add `@tasks.loop(minutes=5) _orphan_sweep_loop` to `PaymentUICog`. The loop body calls the already-defined `_reconcile_admin_chat_orphans()`. Start in `cog_load` after the initial one-shot call at line immediately following the existing sweep invocation; stop in `cog_unload`. Wrap the loop body in try/except so a transient DB error doesn't kill the task. This ensures stranded `awaiting_admin_init` entries are cleaned up within 5 minutes during normal uptime, not just on restart. Add `test_orphan_sweep_loop_cancels_stale_awaiting_admin_init_during_uptime` to validate.",
    "Document the 5-minute staleness cutoff and the uptime sweep loop in the `/docs/runbook-payments.md` if that file is in scope of the polish inventory (it is \u2014 Rev-5 assumption list confirms it's preserved).",
    "If implementation reveals that 5 minutes is too aggressive during transient slow startups, bump the cutoff to 10 minutes (still short enough to resolve batch crashes within a single admin session).",
    "The `awaiting_admin_init` state is a transient internal status that exists only during the atomic batch insert \u2192 per-entry fan-out window. A recipient observing their intent in this state is expected to be rare (requires the bot to be mid-fan-out at the moment of the query). The uptime sweep loop bounds this to <5 minutes.",

No cross-cog dependency. main.py, tests/test_payment_cog_split.py, and tests/test_scheduler.py are NOT modified. A regression test `test_payment_ui_cog_loads_without_admin_chat_cog` validates this.",

After the atomic insert, verified entries iterate through _gate_existing_intent (shared with test-receipt-positive) to transition to awaiting_admin_approval with final_payment_id set and fire admin DMs. This satisfies the brief's test_initiate_batch_payment_atomic_all_or_none.",

  "robustness": "standard",
  "signals": {
    "iteration": 5,
    "idea": "Identity-based routing for admin_chat with pending-recipient state machine, batch payment creation, admin DM approval gate, auto-skip test for verified wallets, and wallet upsert tooling.\n\nARCHITECTURAL CHANGE \u2014 identity-based router replaces content-based message classification. After this megaplan lands, admin_chat message handling routes entirely by WHO the author is, not by WHAT they said:\n\n1. If author is ADMIN_USER_ID \u2192 full agent path (Claude with all tools including new ones). The agent has access to query tools (new, read-only) and action tools (new initiate_payment batch variant, new upsert_wallet_for_user). System prompt softly guides the agent to use query tools for state questions (not strict enforcement \u2014 the admin can correct hallucinations; money cannot move without an explicit tool call so the safety is structural).\n\n2. If author has an active non-terminal row in admin_payment_intents (a pending payment recipient) \u2192 pure state machine. No LLM in the loop. The state machine reads the message text and routes based on the intent's current state. Two states matter:\n   - awaiting_wallet: parse message for a Solana-address-shaped string (base58, 32-44 chars, proper byte length). If found, register the wallet (verified_at=NULL), trigger test payment. If not found, ignore silently (no spamming).\n   - awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation (NEW STATE): parse message for confirmation keywords ('confirmed', 'received', 'got it', 'yes', 'yep', 'confirm', '\ud83d\udc4d'), negative keywords ('no', 'didnt', 'not received', 'missing', 'nothing'), or ambiguous. On confirmation \u2192 mark wallet_registry.verified_at=now, transition intent to queue the real payment. On negative \u2192 tag admin in thread with full context (tx signature, recipient wallet, intent id). On ambiguous \u2192 ignore the first ambiguous message; tag admin on the second.\n\n3. Otherwise \u2192 fall through (the admin_chat cog does nothing; other cogs like grants/gating/logging handle what they handle).\n\nRECIPIENT-FACING FLOW CHANGES:\n\n- First-ever payment to a new wallet: recipient posts wallet (1 text interaction) \u2192 bot sends 0.002 SOL test \u2192 bot posts 'Please check your wallet and reply confirmed when you see it' \u2192 recipient replies with confirmation text (1 text interaction) \u2192 state machine marks wallet verified \u2192 real payment auto-queues \u2192 admin DM approval gate fires \u2192 admin clicks \u2192 real payment sends. RECIPIENT TOTAL: 2 text interactions.\n- Subsequent payments to a now-verified wallet: skip test entirely. Bot creates real payment \u2192 admin DM approval \u2192 admin clicks \u2192 send. RECIPIENT TOTAL: 0 interactions.\n- Drop the existing PaymentConfirmView button for admin_chat real payments. The admin DM is now the sole real-payment gate. PaymentUICog.send_confirmation_request should no longer fire for producer='admin_chat' real payments. It still fires for grants (grants is unaffected by this megaplan; keeps its own flow).\n\nNEW TOOLS FOR THE ADMIN AGENT:\n\nA) query_payment_state(payment_id=None, user_id=None) \u2192 returns canonical DB state for a payment or all recent payments for a user. Read-only.\nB) query_wallet_state(user_id) \u2192 returns wallet_registry rows for a user (address, verified_at, created_at). Read-only.\nC) list_recent_payments(producer=None, user_id=None, limit=20) \u2192 returns recent payment_requests rows with redacted wallets. Read-only.\nD) initiate_batch_payment(payments=[{recipient_user_id, amount_sol, reason?}, ...]) \u2192 creates N admin_payment_intents atomically in a single DB transaction. Each flows through the normal per-payment mechanical pipeline: if wallet unverified, runs the test \u2192 receipt confirmation cycle; once verified, real payment auto-queues; admin DM approval gate fires individually PER payment (one DM per payment, not per batch). If any intent creation fails, roll back all.\nE) upsert_wallet_for_user(user_id, wallet_address, chain='solana', reason?) \u2192 admin-initiated wallet registration. Creates or updates wallet_registry with verified_at=NULL (verification still requires a test payment round-trip). On success, DMs the admin with a confirmation receipt ('wallet for @user set to 8ThP...xyz1, will be verified on next payment'). Does NOT mark verified_at.\n\nADMIN DM APPROVAL GATE:\n\n- Fires ONLY for real (is_test=False) payments under producer='admin_chat'. Grants is unaffected.\n- One DM per payment (even for batches \u2014 16 payments = 16 DMs). The admin clicks Confirm on each.\n- DM message format: clear amount, recipient, wallet (redacted), intent_id, jump link to original thread.\n- On admin click: edit original DM to '\u2705 approved', send follow-up DM 'payment confirmed, sending', post reply in the intent's thread 'Payment sent to @user', delete stale flow messages in the thread via the existing safe_delete_messages primitive.\n- New AdminApprovalView persistent view class, registered on bot startup for recovery across restarts.\n- If admin DM delivery fails (discord.Forbidden, rate limit) \u2192 fail-closed, payment stays in awaiting_admin_approval, log at ERROR level. Do not auto-fall-back to public channel (assumption: DM delivery is reliable in practice; failure is rare enough to be a manual-ops event).\n\nAUTO-SKIP TEST FOR VERIFIED WALLETS:\n\n- When the admin initiates a payment via initiate_payment or initiate_batch_payment, and the recipient has a wallet_registry row with verified_at IS NOT NULL, skip the test payment creation entirely.\n- Go straight to creating the real payment row, auto-queue, trigger admin DM approval.\n- First-time and verified paths share the admin DM approval gate.\n\nHALLUCINATION FIX (softer than originally proposed):\n\n- Add the three query tools (query_payment_state, query_wallet_state, list_recent_payments) to the agent's tool palette.\n- Update the admin agent's system prompt with a new section: 'For questions about the state of payments or wallets, call query_payment_state / query_wallet_state / list_recent_payments FIRST. Do not claim state from memory or channel context.'\n- This is guidance, not enforcement. The agent CAN still hallucinate, but the operator (the admin) is the only audience, can correct, and money cannot move without an explicit tool call (which is visible and auditable).\n- Do NOT add a cog-layer rejection of replies that make state claims without query tool calls \u2014 the user specifically said not to require tool calls.\n\nWHAT THIS MEGAPLAN IS NOT TOUCHING (preserve existing invariants):\n\n- solana_client.py / solana_provider.py \u2014 the rebroadcast loop, priority fees, status.err check, RPC unreachable handling all stay.\n- Hardening pass inventory: per-user-per-day caps, canonical wallet swap defense, idempotency collision detection, WalletUpdateBlockedError, RLS comment, prompt-injection hardening in admin_chat/tools.py (wallet lookups only from wallet_registry), null-recipient fail-closed in PaymentService.confirm_payment.\n- Cleanup inventory: PaymentActor/PRODUCER_FLOWS registry, tx_signature_history column, PaymentWorkerCog + PaymentUICog split, safe_delete_messages primitive.\n- Polish inventory: reconcile_with_chain, dynamic priority fees, /payment-resolve slash command, provider name migration, docs/payments.md, docs/runbook-payments.md, scripts/check_payment_invariants.py, structured tx_confirm_decision logging.\n- Grants cog \u2014 completely unaffected. Grants uses its own thread-scoped listener, has its own flow (test auto-confirmed by bot, recipient clicks button for real payment), and this megaplan does not touch it.\n- The 'approved members can chat with bot' feature \u2014 the _can_user_message_bot path in admin_chat_cog. Under identity-based routing, approved-member messages are no longer forwarded to the agent unless the member is ALSO the ADMIN_USER_ID. This is an intentional scoping decision \u2014 the admin is the sole human who can conversationally interact with the agent. Document this in the plan explicitly so no one is surprised.\n\nSUCCESS CRITERIA:\n\n- Identity router correctly classifies admin, pending recipient, and fallthrough based solely on author identity.\n- Pending recipient state machine handles awaiting_wallet and awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation deterministically with no LLM calls.\n- Test-receipt confirmation flow: recipient's text acknowledgment is the sole gate for marking wallet_registry.verified_at (not on-chain confirmation alone).\n- Negative / ambiguous recipient responses escalate to the admin via thread tag, not silent failure.\n- 24-hour timeout on awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation \u2192 escalate to manual_review + admin DM.\n- New tools (query_*, initiate_batch_payment, upsert_wallet_for_user) are admin-only and correctly scoped.\n- initiate_batch_payment creates all intents atomically or none (DB transaction).\n- Auto-skip test path correctly bypasses the test phase for already-verified wallets.\n- Admin DM approval gate fires exactly once per real admin_chat payment, never for tests, never for grants.\n- AdminApprovalView is persistent-safe across bot restarts.\n- PaymentConfirmView button no longer fires for admin_chat real payments (grants-only now).\n- No regression in existing hardening/cleanup/polish invariants \u2014 all existing tests pass.\n- New tests cover: identity router branches, state machine state transitions, wallet regex parsing, confirmation keyword matching, batch tool atomicity, upsert_wallet_for_user admin check, auto-skip verified path, admin DM approval view persistence and click handling.",
    "significant_flags": 19,

        "severity": "significant",
        "status": "open"
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-017",
        "concern": "Payment UI recovery: Revision 5 treats stale `awaiting_admin_init` cleanup as if it will happen within 5 minutes, but `_reconcile_admin_chat_orphans()` is only scheduled from `PaymentUICog.cog_load()`, so stranded verified-batch intents remain active until restart or manual intervention.",
        "category": "correctness",
        "severity": "significant",
        "status": "open"
      }

    "resolved_flags": [
      {
        "id": "FLAG-001",
        "concern": "Payment UI restart recovery: `PaymentConfirmView` will still be re-registered for admin_chat real payments unless the pending-confirmation recovery query/path is filtered, so the old recipient gate survives restarts.",
        "resolution": "Addressed all 19 gate flags in a focused revision (no re-scope). Key fixes: (1) New Step 3 updates `producer_flows.py` to add `ADMIN_DM` to `admin_chat.real_confirmed_by` \u2014 reuses the **existing** `PaymentActorKind.ADMIN_DM` (not a new `ADMIN_CLICK`). (2) New Step 4 filters the `PaymentConfirmView` restart-recovery pass to skip admin_chat real payments via a new db_handler helper. (3) Step 6 drops `_is_directed_at_bot` gating for ADMIN_USER_ID \u2014 admin messages reach the agent unconditionally (plain channel messages included). (4) Step 5 replaces the whitespace-only wallet parser with a tolerant regex that strips markdown/punctuation delimiters before validating candidates. (5) Step 10 click handler posts \"queued for sending\" (not \"Payment sent\") and defers the sent-confirmation to the existing `handle_payment_result` worker-completion path. (6) Step 1.3 adds a second `receipt_prompt_message_id` column so safe-delete can clear BOTH the wallet prompt AND the receipt prompt. (7) Step 13 verified-wallet batch entries now skip `awaiting_test` entirely \u2014 they land directly in `awaiting_admin_approval` at intent-creation time. (8) Step 15 adds the five new tool names to `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS` so the module-level exhaustiveness assert doesn't fire; Step 16 extends `agent.py`'s `admin_user_id` injection to cover `initiate_batch_payment` and `upsert_wallet_for_user`. (9) Step 2.5 excludes `manual_review` from active-intent lookups AND new Step 20 adds a `resolve_admin_intent` tool as the operator resolution surface \u2014 `manual_review` intents no longer block future initiations and admin can cancel/complete them explicitly. (10) Step 1.4 updates the unique partial index to exclude `manual_review`. Rewrote Execution Order note to call out the Phase 2 triple as a single interlocked change. Added new tests for every flag: producer_flows authorization, restart-recovery filter, tolerant wallet parser, \"queued not sent\" click handler, both-prompt safe-delete, batch verified-skip, ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS exhaustiveness, admin_user_id injection for new tools, manual_review non-blocking, resolve_admin_intent."
      },

      {
        "id": "FLAG-004",
        "concern": "Admin-chat tool plumbing: the new `resolve_admin_intent` tool is added as admin-only, but the revised caller-side `admin_user_id` injection snippet does not include it, so executor-level admin verification is still incomplete.",
        "resolution": "Surgical Revision 3 applying the six targeted fixes from iteration-2 gate feedback without destabilizing Revision 2's resolved work: (1) **Reverted `manual_review` to blocking** \u2014 Step 1.4 no longer modifies the unique partial index; Step 2 no longer modifies `get_active_intent_for_recipient`/`list_active_intents`; explicit note that `has_active_payment_or_intent` is also untouched. Closes FLAG-010, scope-1, scope-2, all_locations-2, callers-3. (2) **`resolve_admin_intent` accepts cancel-only** \u2014 dropped the `resolution` parameter entirely; the tool always writes `cancelled`. Added `test_resolve_admin_intent_rejects_completed`. Closes FLAG-011, correctness-2. (3) **Verified-wallet crash-safe ordering** \u2014 Step 9.1 now spells out explicit A/B/C ordering: `request_payment` first, then one atomic `update_admin_payment_intent({'status': 'awaiting_admin_approval', 'final_payment_id': pid})`, then `_send_admin_approval_dm`. No intent can exist with `awaiting_admin_approval` + `final_payment_id=NULL`. Step 11 and Step 13 use the helper and use `awaiting_wallet` as a pre-insert placeholder (never write `awaiting_admin_approval` without a payment id). Added `test_verified_wallet_crash_safety_payment_row_before_intent_status`. Closes FLAG-008, correctness-1, callers-2. (4) **State-aware restart filter** \u2014 Step 4 now filters only admin_chat real payments whose linked intent is in `awaiting_admin_approval`. Legacy admin_chat rows with intent in `awaiting_confirmation` still get `PaymentConfirmView` on restart. New `get_pending_confirmation_admin_chat_intents_by_payment` helper in Step 2.5. Added `test_restart_filter_state_aware` with three scenarios. Closes FLAG-001 (reaffirmed), FLAG-007, issue_hints-2, all_locations-1. (5) **Explicit admin DM in `upsert_wallet_for_user`** \u2014 Step 14 executor now calls `bot.fetch_user(ADMIN_USER_ID).send(...)` directly, wrapped in try/except for Forbidden/HTTPException (DM is confirmation only, DB write still succeeds). Added `test_upsert_wallet_for_user_dms_admin_explicitly`. Closes FLAG-009, issue_hints-1. (6) **`resolve_admin_intent` added to `_ADMIN_IDENTITY_INJECTED_TOOLS`** \u2014 Step 16 frozenset now includes it; Step 15 adds it to `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS`. Added `test_resolve_admin_intent_admin_injection`. Closes FLAG-004, callers-1. Narrative rewrite in Overview explains the six fixes up front; carryover flags from iteration 1 (issue_hints-3, correctness-3, callers-3) remain addressed. Success criteria updated to match: removed criterion \"db_handler.get_active_intent_for_recipient and list_active_intents exclude manual_review\" (no longer true), replaced with \"manual_review remains a blocking state and admin clears via resolve_admin_intent(cancelled)\"; updated AdminApprovalView/crash-safety criterion; updated upsert_wallet_for_user criterion to mandate explicit DM."
      },

Closes FLAG-010, scope-1, scope-2, all_locations-2, callers-3. Closes FLAG-011, correctness-2. (3) **Verified-wallet crash-safe ordering** \u2014 Step 9.1 now spells out explicit A/B/C ordering: `request_payment` first, then one atomic `update_admin_payment_intent({'status': 'awaiting_admin_approval', 'final_payment_id': pid})`, then `_send_admin_approval_dm`. Step 11 and Step 13 use the helper and use `awaiting_wallet` as a pre-insert placeholder (never write `awaiting_admin_approval` without a payment id). Added `test_verified_wallet_crash_safety_payment_row_before_intent_status`. Closes FLAG-008, correctness-1, callers-2.       {
        "id": "FLAG-012",
        "concern": "Admin-chat approval flow: Revision 3 reintroduces `PaymentConfirmView` for NEW-flow admin_chat real payments in its crash-safety fallback, which conflicts with the requirement that recipient confirmation no longer fire for admin_chat real payments.",
        "resolution": "Revision 4 applies three surgical fixes to address iteration-3 gate flags: (1) **Eliminated the `awaiting_wallet` placeholder for verified-wallet fast path.** Step 9 now defines TWO helpers: `_gate_existing_intent` (for test-receipt positive, intent already exists \u2192 request_payment \u2192 atomic update with status+final_payment_id \u2192 admin DM) and `_gate_fresh_intent_atomic` (for auto-skip, no intent yet \u2192 duplicate check \u2192 request_payment \u2192 atomic INSERT of intent with status='awaiting_admin_approval' AND final_payment_id in one call \u2192 admin DM). Steps 11 and 13 use the new helper instead of placeholder state. No intent is EVER observed in `awaiting_wallet` during the verified-wallet path. Closes FLAG-013, correctness-1, callers-1, issue_hints-2, all_locations-2. (2) **Added a startup orphan-payment reconciliation sweep** in new Step 18 (`_reconcile_admin_chat_orphan_payments`). Runs BEFORE `_register_pending_confirmation_views` via a new `cog_load` ordering dependency from PaymentUICog \u2192 AdminChatCog. Four cases: auto-skip crash (no intent \u2192 cancel orphan via existing `cancel_payment`); test-receipt crash (intent in awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation \u2192 complete the intent update + fire admin DM); legacy `awaiting_confirmation` \u2192 leave alone for PaymentConfirmView re-registration; already wired \u2192 leave alone for AdminApprovalView re-registration. Crash-safety no longer requires falling back to PaymentConfirmView for new-flow admin_chat real payments. Closes FLAG-012, issue_hints-1, scope-1. (3) **`resolve_admin_intent` now cascade-cancels linked `payment_requests`** via the existing `db_handler.cancel_payment(payment_id, guild_id)` primitive (mirrors `admin_chat_cog.py:616-620`). Cascade runs BEFORE the intent update; refuses to act if any linked payment is in `submitted`/`confirmed`/`manual_hold` status (operator must use `/payment-resolve` first). Closes FLAG-014, correctness-2, scope-2, all_locations-1, callers-2. Also added `find_admin_chat_intent_by_payment_id` DB helper (Step 2.6) as a fallback lookup for the orphan sweep. Added four new orphan-sweep tests and three new resolve-cascade tests in Step 21. Updated success criteria: removed the criterion about `awaiting_wallet` placeholder (no longer exists); added an explicit \"no placeholder state\" criterion; updated the crash-safety criterion to describe the sweep-based recovery rather than PaymentConfirmView fallback; added a resolve-cascade criterion."

        "id": "FLAG-014",

        "resolution": "Revision 4 applies three surgical fixes to address iteration-3 gate flags: (1) **Eliminated the `awaiting_wallet` placeholder for verified-wallet fast path.** Step 9 now defines TWO helpers: `_gate_existing_intent` (for test-receipt positive, intent already exists \u2192 request_payment \u2192 atomic update with status+final_payment_id \u2192 admin DM) and `_gate_fresh_intent_atomic` (for auto-skip, no intent yet \u2192 duplicate check \u2192 request_payment \u2192 atomic INSERT of intent with status='awaiting_admin_approval' AND final_payment_id in one call \u2192 admin DM).       },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-015",
        "concern": "Payment UI startup ordering: Revision 4 makes `PaymentUICog.cog_load()` depend on an `AdminChatCog` orphan-sweep accessor, but the current bootstrap and test harness load `PaymentUICog` before or without `AdminChatCog`, so the plan misses required bootstrap/test updates or a fallback path.",
        "resolution": "Revision 5 applies two surgical fixes to resolve the iteration-4 concerns: (1) **Inverted the orphan-sweep dependency** \u2014 moved `_reconcile_admin_chat_orphans` OUT of `AdminChatCog` and INTO `PaymentUICog` itself. The sweep only needs `self.db_handler`, `self.payment_service`, `self.bot`, and `self._send_admin_approval_dm`, all already accessible from the UI cog. This eliminates the cross-cog ordering problem entirely: no `bot.admin_chat_cog.reconcile_orphan_payments_for_ui_cog()` accessor, no `main.py` bootstrap reorder, no test harness changes, no `FakeBot` additions. Added a critical regression test `test_payment_ui_cog_loads_without_admin_chat_cog` in `test_payment_cog_split.py` that validates `PaymentUICog.cog_load()` works with a `FakeBot` that has no `admin_chat_cog` attribute. Closes FLAG-015, correctness (v4), all_locations (v4), callers (v4). (2) **Restored unified atomic batch semantics via a new transient `awaiting_admin_init` state.** All `initiate_batch_payment` entries (verified + unverified) now go through ONE `create_admin_payment_intents_batch` call \u2014 true all-or-none single-DB-call semantics matching the brief's fail_to_pass test. Verified entries are inserted with `status='awaiting_admin_init'`; unverified with `status='awaiting_wallet'`. `awaiting_admin_init` is added to the CHECK constraint (Step 1), silently ignored by the state-machine dispatch (Step 7 \u2014 new unit test `test_state_machine_silently_ignores_awaiting_admin_init` asserts posting a Solana address never reopens the test path), and handled by the orphan sweep as a new case (e): stale `awaiting_admin_init` intents older than 5 minutes with no `final_payment_id` are cancelled (fail-closed, no payment_requests cleanup needed because no payment row was ever created). After the atomic batch insert succeeds, verified entries iterate through the existing `_gate_existing_intent` helper (unified with the test-receipt-positive path) to create their payment and transition to `awaiting_admin_approval`. Added `test_initiate_batch_payment_verified_fan_out_transitions_to_awaiting_admin_approval` and `test_initiate_batch_payment_fan_out_crash_leaves_awaiting_admin_init_for_sweep`. Closes FLAG-016, issue_hints (v4), scope (v4). `_gate_fresh_intent_atomic` stays in place for the SINGLE `initiate_payment` verified path (no batch wrapper); batch exclusively uses `_gate_existing_intent` for fan-out. No changes to Phases 1-3 structure, no changes to `main.py`, no changes to `test_payment_cog_split.py` or `test_scheduler.py` harnesses."
      },

        "id": "FLAG-016",
        "concern": "Admin-chat batch payments: Revision 4 weakens `initiate_batch_payment` from an all-or-none batch into split semantics where verified entries are processed per-entry and can partially succeed or orphan/cancel later, which diverges from the brief and stated test expectation that the batch is atomic across all requested payments.",

After the atomic batch insert succeeds, verified entries iterate through the existing `_gate_existing_intent` helper (unified with the test-receipt-positive path) to create their payment and transition to `awaiting_admin_approval`. Closes FLAG-016, issue_hints (v4), scope (v4). `_gate_fresh_intent_atomic` stays in place for the SINGLE `initiate_payment` verified path (no batch wrapper); batch exclusively uses `_gate_existing_intent` for fan-out. Fix is trivial (~10 lines) and carried forward as an explicit execution-time directive in warnings: add `@tasks.loop(minutes=5) _orphan_sweep_loop` to PaymentUICog calling the existing sweep method. Does not require another iteration round \u2014 all design-level questions are resolved."

      "flag_id": "issue_hints",
      "action": "dispute",
      "evidence": "Brief text: 'initiate_batch_payment(payments=[...]) \u2192 creates N admin_payment_intents atomically in a single DB transaction. Each flows through the normal per-payment mechanical pipeline: if wallet unverified, runs the test \u2192 receipt confirmation cycle; once verified, real payment auto-queues; admin DM approval gate fires individually PER payment'. The brief explicitly acknowledges per-payment pipeline processing AFTER atomic intent creation, which is exactly what Rev-5 implements. `awaiting_admin_init` is a transient internal state that exists only during the atomic insert \u2192 per-entry fan-out phase within the same tool invocation; from the outside observer the intent ends up in awaiting_admin_approval as the brief requires. The 'verified wallet goes straight to real-payment/admin-approval at intent creation time' language in the earlier part of the brief applies to single initiate_payment (handled correctly by `_gate_fresh_intent_atomic`), not batch.",

        Requirements:
- Implement the intent, not just the text.
- Adapt if repository reality contradicts the plan.
- Report deviations explicitly.
- Do not over-engineer beyond what the plan prescribes — no str() wraps, .get() fallbacks, or try/except guards unless the plan called for them or you found a concrete reason.
- Do NOT fix unrelated issues you encounter (e.g., dependency compatibility, Python version workarounds). Only change files directly needed for the task. If tests need updating, only update tests that are directly related to your fix.
- If you cannot build the project from source (e.g., C extension compilation failures), report the build failure explicitly. Do NOT fall back to testing against an installed or cached package — that tests the wrong codebase and produces false positives.

- If tests fail, read the traceback carefully. Diagnose WHY — don't just retry. Common causes: wrong function/method used, missing import, incorrect type, edge case not handled. Fix the root cause, then re-run.
- When verifying changes, run the entire test file or module (e.g., `pytest tests/test_foo.py`), not individual test functions. Individual tests miss regressions in the same module.
- finalize.json includes baseline_test_failures — a list of test IDs that were already failing before your changes. If a test fails and its ID appears in baseline_test_failures, it is pre-existing — do not scope-creep into fixing it. If baseline_test_failures is null, the baseline could not be captured; use your judgment but err on the side of assuming failures are regressions. You MUST still re-run the FULL test suite with your changes applied — pre-existing failures do not excuse skipping verification. Never narrow to individual test functions and stop.

- SC1 (T1): Does the migration use idempotent DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS / ADD CONSTRAINT, include all four new statuses (awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation, awaiting_admin_approval, awaiting_admin_init, manual_review) plus all preexisting statuses including legacy awaiting_confirmation, and leave the unique partial index untouched?
- SC2 (T2): Does create_admin_payment_intents_batch use a single Supabase .insert([...]) call (atomic per PostgREST request), and does list_stale_awaiting_admin_init_intents filter on both status='awaiting_admin_init' AND updated_at < cutoff?
- SC3 (T3): Was PaymentActorKind.ADMIN_DM added to admin_chat.real_confirmed_by without introducing a new enum value, and does grants.real_confirmed_by remain unchanged?
- SC4 (T4): Does the state-aware filter correctly handle three scenarios — admin_chat real with intent in awaiting_admin_approval/awaiting_admin_init (skip), admin_chat real with intent in legacy awaiting_confirmation (re-register), grants (always re-register) — and run AFTER the orphan sweep?
- SC5 (T5): Does _classify_confirmation use whole-word case-insensitive matching with the emoji '👍' as a substring fallback, and does _parse_wallet_from_text strip markdown delimiters before validating via is_valid_solana_address?
- SC6 (T6): Is on_message a strict three-way switch on author.id with no _is_directed_at_bot or _can_user_message_bot gates for the admin path, and is the _check_pending_payment_reply call deleted?
- SC7 (T7): Does the dispatch silently return for awaiting_admin_init (and all other non-routable states), and is the test asserting that posting a Solana address in awaiting_admin_init does NOT call _handle_wallet_received in place?
- SC8 (T8): Is the send_confirmation_request call in admin_chat_cog.py deleted, and does the test-success branch transition to awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation + store receipt_prompt_message_id without creating the real payment?
- SC9 (T9): Does _gate_existing_intent execute steps in A→B→C order (request_payment first, then ONE atomic update with status+final_payment_id, then admin DM), and does _gate_fresh_intent_atomic INSERT the intent with status='awaiting_admin_approval' AND final_payment_id in the SAME create call?
- SC10 (T10): Does AdminApprovalView use timeout=None, custom_id 'payment_admin_approve:{payment_id}' (no collision with payment_confirm), post 'queued for sending' (NOT 'Payment sent'), and call safe_delete_messages with BOTH prompt ids? Does _send_admin_approval_dm fail-closed on Forbidden (return None, log ERROR, intent stays in awaiting_admin_approval)?
- SC11 (T11): Does execute_initiate_payment call _gate_fresh_intent_atomic on the verified-wallet path with no test payment created, and is the unverified path unchanged?
- SC12 (T12): Are all three query tools read-only (no DB writes), do they apply wallet redaction, and do they apply the pop-and-warn LLM-wallet injection guard?
- SC13 (T13): Does initiate_batch_payment use a SINGLE create_admin_payment_intents_batch call for ALL entries (verified inserted as awaiting_admin_init, unverified as awaiting_wallet) with all-or-none semantics, and does verified fan-out call _gate_existing_intent (NOT _gate_fresh_intent_atomic)?
- SC14 (T14): Does upsert_wallet_for_user check admin via injected admin_user_id, validate the wallet via is_valid_solana_address, leave verified_at NULL, surface WalletUpdateBlockedError, and EXPLICITLY DM the admin via bot.fetch_user wrapped in try/except?
- SC15 (T15): Are all six new tools added to ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS (asserting passes), and does _ADMIN_IDENTITY_INJECTED_TOOLS include initiate_payment, initiate_batch_payment, upsert_wallet_for_user, and resolve_admin_intent?
- SC16 (T16): Does PaymentUICog._reconcile_admin_chat_orphans use ONLY self.db_handler/self.payment_service/self.bot/self._send_admin_approval_dm with no reference to bot.admin_chat_cog, handle all six cases including the new stale awaiting_admin_init case (e), AND is the @tasks.loop(minutes=5) periodic sweep wired in cog_load (with try/except in the body) per the gate's execution-time directive?
- SC17 (T17): Is the 24h sweep at 15-minute intervals, does it transition stale awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation intents to manual_review, and is it started/stopped in cog_load/cog_unload with try/except?
- SC18 (T18): Is resolve_admin_intent cancel-only (no resolution parameter), does it cascade-cancel linked test_payment_id AND final_payment_id via cancel_payment, refuse non-cancellable linked payments (submitted/confirmed/manual_hold), and explicitly DM the admin?
- SC19 (T19): Are tests mocking _classify_payment_reply removed; do new tests assert zero Anthropic calls on the state-machine path; does test_payment_ui_cog_loads_without_admin_chat_cog instantiate PaymentUICog with a FakeBot lacking admin_chat_cog and complete cog_load without AttributeError; does test_initiate_batch_payment_atomic_all_or_none verify the single-DB-call semantic; does test_initiate_batch_payment_fan_out_crash assert cancellation via the orphan sweep; and is test_payment_cog_split.py harness left UNMODIFIED (only new tests added)?
- SC20 (T20): Did pytest test_admin_payments.py test_payment_cog_split.py test_scheduler.py pass with -x; did all pass_to_pass invariant suites remain green; did the throwaway repro script verify both the unverified test-receipt round-trip AND the verified single-initiate atomic insert; was the script deleted; and were no tests skipped/xfailed?
Watch items to keep visible during execution:
- GATE WARNING (execution-time required, T16): Add @tasks.loop(minutes=5) _orphan_sweep_loop to PaymentUICog wrapping _reconcile_admin_chat_orphans. This is the FLAG-017 fix — without it, stranded awaiting_admin_init intents only clear at restart. Start in cog_load AFTER initial sweep, stop in cog_unload, try/except the loop body.
- DO NOT touch solana_client.py, solana_provider.py, grants_cog.py, main.py, the existing harnesses in tests/test_payment_cog_split.py or tests/test_scheduler.py. The Rev-5 dependency-inversion design exists specifically to avoid these.
- awaiting_admin_init is a transient internal state. The state-machine dispatch (T7) MUST silently ignore it — a recipient posting a Solana address while in this state must NEVER reopen the wallet-collection or test-payment path. There is a dedicated unit test for this in T19; it is the highest-risk regression vector.
- Atomicity contract for initiate_batch_payment (T13): one create_admin_payment_intents_batch call for ALL entries (verified + unverified). On any validation failure or DB rejection, ZERO rows persist. Per-entry fan-out via _gate_existing_intent is a separate non-atomic phase covered by the orphan sweep.
- _gate_existing_intent ordering (T9): A) request_payment, B) ONE update with both status='awaiting_admin_approval' AND final_payment_id, C) admin DM. Crash between A-B → orphan sweep case (d) recovers. Crash between B-C → restart re-registers AdminApprovalView. No window can leave the intent in awaiting_admin_approval with final_payment_id NULL.
- _gate_fresh_intent_atomic (T9, single initiate path only): atomic INSERT of intent with status='awaiting_admin_approval' AND final_payment_id in the SAME create call (client-side uuid4 for intent_id). No placeholder state. Crash before insert → orphan sweep case (a) cancels payment.
- manual_review remains BLOCKING. Do NOT modify get_active_intent_for_recipient, list_active_intents, has_active_payment_or_intent, or the unique partial index. Admin clears manual_review via resolve_admin_intent (T18) which cascade-cancels linked payments.
- AdminApprovalView click handler (T10) posts 'queued for sending' — NOT 'Payment sent'. The existing handle_payment_result emits the sent confirmation later when the worker completes. confirm_payment only transitions pending_confirmation → queued.
- safe_delete_messages must clear BOTH prompt_message_id AND receipt_prompt_message_id. The receipt_prompt_message_id column is added in T1 specifically for this.
- PaymentConfirmView must NEVER fire for new-flow admin_chat real payments. The state-aware filter (T4) skips them; only legacy awaiting_confirmation rows are re-registered. Test test_restart_filter_state_aware verifies all three scenarios.
- Anthropic API calls: the pending-recipient state machine must make ZERO LLM calls. Tests in T19 use a mock that raises on any anthropic client call to enforce this invariant.
- verified_at is set ONLY via mark_wallet_verified, ONLY after on-chain test confirmation AND recipient text-ack. upsert_wallet_for_user (T14) creates rows with verified_at=NULL.
- LLM-wallet injection guard: every new tool taking payment params (initiate_batch_payment, query tools) must apply the existing pop-and-warn pattern from execute_initiate_payment. Wallets are sourced ONLY from wallet_registry, never from LLM-provided strings.
- 24h timeout sweep (T17) lives in AdminChatCog at 15-minute intervals — above the ≥5 minute constraint. Separate from T16's 5-minute orphan sweep loop in PaymentUICog.
- Custom_id collision check: f'payment_admin_approve:{payment_id}' must not collide with f'payment_confirm:{payment_id}'. Grep to confirm.
- Identity router (T6): admin messages reach the agent UNCONDITIONALLY — drop _is_directed_at_bot gating for ADMIN_USER_ID. _can_user_message_bot, MEMBER_SYSTEM_PROMPT, _is_directed_at_bot become dead code (do NOT delete).
- Hardening invariants preserved: per-user-per-day caps, canonical wallet swap defense, idempotency collision detection, WalletUpdateBlockedError, RLS, prompt-injection hardening, null-recipient fail-closed.
- Debt watch: anthropic client may not be initialized when interceptor fires before _ensure_agent — ensure the new identity router doesn't reintroduce this race. The state machine path uses zero LLM calls so this debt is naturally avoided there.
- Test churn expected in test_admin_payments.py (~1350 lines). Tests mocking _classify_payment_reply must be removed entirely; tests asserting the awaiting_confirmation→final-payment transition switch to awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation; PaymentConfirmView expectations for admin_chat move to AdminApprovalView.
Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] admin-chat-classification: anthropic client may not be initialized when interceptor fires before _ensure_agent. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] admin-payments-intent-state: button confirmation path does not directly update admin_payment_intents — intent sync happens indirectly via handle_payment_result on terminal state. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] admin-payments-intent-state: no direct bridge from paymentconfirmview to admin_payment_intents. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] admin-payments-intent-state: awaiting_admin_init stale cleanup only runs at cog_load, not periodically, so stranded verified-batch intents could remain active until restart (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] admin-payments-intent-state: scheduling glue for periodic _reconcile_admin_chat_orphans invocation is not in the plan (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] admin-payments-intent-state: awaiting_admin_init in the active set has broader effects on active-intent callers across the repo until restart (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] db-handler-social-publications: mixing fluent client, raw sql, and rpc access patterns in one subsystem increases maintenance surface. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] db-handler-supabase-infrastructure: supabase rpc is untested in this codebase and may require dashboard configuration. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] payment-service-confirmation: success criterion bundles null-recipient dm wiring with cap-breach/collision dm wiring; step 1 only does log+return-none for null-recipient. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] social-channel-routes: route management tooling is absent, leaving the routing system inert until manual db inserts. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
